    ✔ no application role can UPDATE, DELETE or TRUNCATE a receipt; app_worker cannot UPDATE an anchor (45.02614ms)
    ✔ a receipt cannot name another organisation than its anchor, and PUBLIC and app_owner hold no right on the table (12.543378ms)
  ▶ audit.archive_anchors
    ✔ archives every anchor once: key, version and SHA-256 are on the receipt; a second run puts nothing (5.061951ms)
    ✔ an object-store error leaves the anchor for the next run and the lag grows; the retry archives it (10.481223ms)
    ✔ 412 with an equal body inserts a receipt with the existing version (crash between put and insert) (17.625658ms)
    ✔ 412 with a different body publishes audit.anchor_mismatch (archive_differs) and inserts nothing (21.944101ms)
    ✔ the task emits AuditAnchorArchiveLagSeconds on every run, 0 when nothing waits (4.0136ms)
  ✔ audit.archive_anchors (59.649337ms)
  ▶ audit.verify_anchors: the archive is the authority
    ✔ untouched data verifies clean with the archive, and the archive was actually read (12.626374ms)
    ✔ tamper: an event row changed and the anchor chain rewritten consistently as owner -> db_differs_from_archive and an alert (86.69788ms)
    ✔ an anchor missing from the database but present in the archive is db_differs_from_archive, unless it is after the restore point (22.010937ms)
    ✔ a deleted receipt is not_archived once the anchor is older than the grace; a fresh anchor is not flagged under the default grace (26.177085ms)
    ✔ a receipt that names a version the archive does not list is not_archived (forged receipt) (12.607908ms)
    ✔ a later version of an archived key is itself a finding (archive_extra_version), judged on the earliest version (13.010446ms)
    ✔ an unreadable archive fails the task after the database checks and the mismatch events; it never reads as clean (18.014378ms)
  ✔ audit.verify_anchors: the archive is the authority (191.682287ms)

▶ notifications on PostgreSQL 17 (BE-06, worker side)
  ▶ the send job
    ✔ e-mail: renders the published version in the person's language, attaches the .ics, stores the vendor reference and no address or text (63.89865ms)
    ✔ language: the person's language when published, else the default; SMS and push render without a subject/with a title (45.608615ms)
    ✔ TC-NT-08: SMS off and e-mail on - the SMS is suppressed with its reason and the provider is never called; e-mail is sent (23.65209ms)
    ✔ suppression reasons: no address, unknown recipient, inactive patient, no contributor, no template (42.711753ms)
    ✔ in-app notices need no vendor and are sent even when every external channel is off (TC-NT-04) (5.235767ms)
    ✔ TC-NT-04: a failing provider is retried by the queue; the failure is an error CLASS; the last attempt publishes delivery_failed; recovery sends with the same token (39.861997ms)
    ✔ a vendor refusal (4xx) is final: failed, no queue retry, delivery_failed published (10.427052ms)
    ✔ quiet hours defer the send to when they end, restore the attempt count and queue the job (23.649846ms)
    ✔ a template that leaks text is stopped at send time by the output scan; the provider is never called (8.41981ms)
    ✔ a render error (a declared value missing) is a final failure, not a retry (7.330142ms)
    ✔ BE06-F1: a merge that re-points the row during the vendor call - sent, failed and deferred outcomes are still recorded (under the survivor) (31.115714ms)
    ✔ a patient merged while the job waited: the job follows the survivor and sends to the survivor's contacts (on_merged = resolve) (10.176281ms)
    ✔ a staff notice runs under the organisation scope and resolves through the principal contributor (7.050337ms)
    ✔ no address, message text or canary reaches a log line (0.167565ms)
  ✔ the send job (320.896119ms)
  ▶ delivery receipts (33.1 notifications.on_receipt, SBR-16)
    ✔ a receipt for a kept notification of a merged patient attaches to the SURVIVOR; a repeat of the event is a no-op (12.52329ms)
    ✔ untrusted payloads: unknown reference, bad shape, bad status, oversized and ambiguous references record nothing (7.116972ms)
    ✔ the inbound handler records through the same path (2.520406ms)
    ✔ the worker role cannot update or delete a receipt, or write one for a patient outside its job scope (2.977033ms)
  ✔ delivery receipts (33.1 notifications.on_receipt, SBR-16) (27.225254ms)
  ▶ channel adapters implement the BE-02 interface
    ✔ argument checks reject header injection, malformed addresses and oversize text without echoing them (0.696399ms)
    ✔ provider errors map to AdapterFailure with the provider's retryability; any other exception is retryable; health never throws (0.371297ms)
